2026-02-24 10:16:31 | Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Database.Command | INF | Executed DbCommand (4ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT f."FileId", (
    SELECT f0."ChangeType"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f0
    WHERE f0."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f0."FileId"
    ORDER BY f0."DetectedAt" DESC
    LIMIT 1) AS "LatestChangeType", COALESCE((
    SELECT f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f1
    WHERE f1."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f1."FileId" AND f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" IS NOT NULL AND f1."ChangeType" IN ('Upload', 'Modified')
    ORDER BY f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" DESC
    LIMIT 1), TIMESTAMPTZ '-infinity') AS "ModifiedTime"
FROM file_change_logs AS f
WHERE f."FileId" IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY f."FileId"

2026-02-24 10:34:46 | Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Database.Command | INF | Executed DbCommand (4ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT f."FileId", (
    SELECT f0."ChangeType"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f0
    WHERE f0."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f0."FileId"
    ORDER BY f0."DetectedAt" DESC
    LIMIT 1) AS "LatestChangeType", COALESCE((
    SELECT f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f1
    WHERE f1."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f1."FileId" AND f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" IS NOT NULL AND f1."ChangeType" IN ('Upload', 'Modified')
    ORDER BY f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" DESC
    LIMIT 1), TIMESTAMPTZ '-infinity') AS "ModifiedTime"
FROM file_change_logs AS f
WHERE f."FileId" IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY f."FileId"

2026-02-24 11:08:58 | Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Database.Command | INF | Executed DbCommand (7ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT f."FileId", (
    SELECT f0."ChangeType"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f0
    WHERE f0."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f0."FileId"
    ORDER BY f0."DetectedAt" DESC
    LIMIT 1) AS "LatestChangeType", COALESCE((
    SELECT f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f1
    WHERE f1."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f1."FileId" AND f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" IS NOT NULL AND f1."ChangeType" IN ('Upload', 'Modified')
    ORDER BY f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" DESC
    LIMIT 1), TIMESTAMPTZ '-infinity') AS "ModifiedTime"
FROM file_change_logs AS f
WHERE f."FileId" IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY f."FileId"

2026-02-24 13:55:59 | Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Database.Command | INF | Executed DbCommand (4ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT f."FileId", (
    SELECT f0."ChangeType"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f0
    WHERE f0."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f0."FileId"
    ORDER BY f0."DetectedAt" DESC
    LIMIT 1) AS "LatestChangeType", COALESCE((
    SELECT f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f1
    WHERE f1."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f1."FileId" AND f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" IS NOT NULL AND f1."ChangeType" IN ('Upload', 'Modified')
    ORDER BY f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" DESC
    LIMIT 1), TIMESTAMPTZ '-infinity') AS "ModifiedTime"
FROM file_change_logs AS f
WHERE f."FileId" IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY f."FileId"

    SELECT f0."ChangeType"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f0
    WHERE f0."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f0."FileId"
    ORDER BY f0."DetectedAt" DESC
    LIMIT 1) AS "LatestChangeType", COALESCE((
    SELECT f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f1
    WHERE f1."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f1."FileId" AND f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" IS NOT NULL AND f1."ChangeType" IN ('Upload', 'Modified')

SELECT f."FileId", (
    SELECT f0."ChangeType"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f0
    WHERE f0."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f0."FileId"
    ORDER BY f0."DetectedAt" DESC
    LIMIT 1) AS "LatestChangeType", COALESCE((
    SELECT f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f1
    WHERE f1."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f1."FileId" AND f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" IS NOT NULL AND f1."ChangeType" IN ('Upload', 'Modified')
    ORDER BY f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" DESC
    LIMIT 1), TIMESTAMPTZ '-infinity') AS "ModifiedTime"
FROM file_change_logs AS f
WHERE f."FileId" IS NOT NULL
